Raw provider OHLC remains unadjusted: no local split/dividend adjustment, total-return synthesis or synthetic bars. `continuity_features.opening_gap` and `previous_close` suppress previous-close interpretation across an invalid price boundary. `historical_early_volume` applies the separate volume flag after rejecting future/incomplete references; a dividend therefore does not erase eligible volume history. `ContinuityContext.segments` uses metadata reset points for material segmentation. `opening_features.opening_observation`, opening range and session VWAP consume a single validated CompletedSession; historical early-volume references are cross-session. Retained tests prove these distinctions. No strategy indicator or feature implementation changed; only the accepted evidence generation label was added.
